# AI Media Scout — 2026-11-06

Expanded creative-AI research produced 42 detailed guides and 46 additional eligible screened discoveries. Older pending entries are completed where source evidence supports a guide; first coverage is not a claim of a new launch. New adjacent fields include acoustic holography and robotic calligraphy. All platforms receive equal consideration; documented tested routes and unsupported or untested ports are stated separately. The initial weekly review was complete for all 1,620 existing tools; today’s records have dated six-check assessments. None were installed or run, and no tool is promoted by stars or a demo alone. Every selected complete software license was reviewed, while separate restricted model weights, proprietary hosts and paid services remain visible. Raw unreviewed candidates remain local; this is a bounded discovery and documentation review, not an exhaustive census. Model-task discovery is bounded to at most 50 recent models per task; these model leads are not software-license approvals. Verified collection: 741/741 repository queries attempted; 741 successful, 0 partial, 0 failed and 125 bounded/truncated. All 20 model tasks were attempted; 0 failed. These are bounded discovery attempts, not an exhaustive census or quality approval. Separate from successful search queries, 31 watchlist fetches and 4 enrichment attempts failed; the detailed warnings remain in the report. A 404/409 is an availability gap, not proof of abandonment or license revocation.

Documentation reviewed today. Tools are not hands-on tested unless explicitly stated. Requirements and performance remain source-specific.

[Source 1](https://opg.optica.org/ao/abstract.cfm?uri=ao-65-10-D69) |
| Fulldome and planetarium AI media | The OpenSpace AI primary paper describes conversational planetarium control in a pilot with professional guides. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.20466) |
| Neural embroidery and stitch design | StitchFoundry’s current site describes embroidery and related design products. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://stitchfoundry.app/) |
| AI printmaking, risograph and editioned art | Imprimatur and StitchFoundry/ScreenFoundry were revisited. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://www.imprimatur.fyi/) |
| AI museum experiences and participatory exhibition media | The primary sources cover the National Museum Norway local-Qwen/BGE semantic-search pilot, ZKM’s open-code context and LinkedCulture’s working prototype. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. This edition separately reviews Relic Ink Collage. [Source 1](https://beta.nasjonalmuseet.no/2026/26/semantic-search-in-the-collection-using-local-ai/) [Source 2](https://zkm.de/en/open-source-code) [Source 3](https://linkedculture.org/about) |
| AI-assisted stop motion and frame animation | Eagle Animation’s primary page does not itself establish an implemented AI role. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://eagle-animation.com/) |
| Neural soundscape ecology and sonic art | The SoundPlot primary paper remains biodiversity-sonification research context. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.12752) |
| Learned anamorphoses and distorted-view art | The actual LookingGlass primary paper was captured directly and describes latent-flow-guided anamorphoses via Laplacian Pyramid Warping. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2504.08902) |
| Neural multiview and glasses-free display art | The primary light-field neural-rendering project describes learned view synthesis. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://light-field-neural-rendering.github.io/) |
| Neural astrophotography and celestial media | The AstroSURE primary abstract remains a no-clean-ground-truth astronomical denoising study with limited domain transfer. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2604.16793) |
| AI tactile graphics and accessible relief media | The primary 2.5D tactile-graphics paper remains research context for generated relief, texture and braille with reported physical studies. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://arxiv.org/abs/2607.22674) |
| Neural underwater photography, film and scene restoration | UDnet’s current primary gallery supplies underwater image/video examples and distinguishes released V1/V2 from optional AI-Max. Today’s primary-source retrieval was compared with the retained snapshot; unchanged content and navigation/count changes are not new software findings. [Source 1](https://alzayats.github.io/UDnet/) |
| Learned acoustic holography and instrument field media | Current primary acoustic-holography research and the complete NAH-SRCNN repository support a guide to learned reconstruction of violin-plate vibration from acoustic pressure. This is a field/visualization workflow; it is not an acoustic-levitation controller. The adjacent robotic/acoustic search remains open-ended. [Source 1](https://eurasip.org/Proceedings/Eusipco/Eusipco2021/pdfs/0000121.pdf) [Source 2](https://github.com/polimi-ispl/nah-srcnn) |
| Learned calligraphy and robotic brush performance | CalliRewrite’s current project page and repository document learned stroke extraction and policy refinement for calligraphic robot performance. Two exported environments and checkpoint requirements are separately disclosed; neither a pretrained result nor robot compatibility establishes a successful physical run on our hardware. HandwritingWithAI did not return a software license. [Source 1](https://luoprojectpage.github.io/callirewrite/) [Source 2](https://github.com/LoYuXr/CalliRewrite) [Source 3](https://github.com/im4vk/HandwritingWithAI) |

## Search and review counts

88 creative fields; 741/741 repository queries attempted; 20/20 model-task queries attempted. 30316 distinct source candidates and 868 model leads. 42 detailed profiles and 46 additional screened discoveries. Source gaps: 0 failed repository queries, 0 partial repository queries, 125 bounded repository queries, 0 failed model-task queries and 2 web ecosystem gaps. Raw search candidates include duplicates of known tools, excluded projects and projects awaiting review; they are not verified recommendations.

## Research beyond GitHub

- **gitlab** · searched: Fresh primary snapshots of AudioStellar, perso-ai, LibreTerrain and Embedded Amp Model were compared with retained versions. They provide current context beyond GitHub, not a claim of a new release or a repeated unchanged entry. No additional GitLab tool completed a new eligibility/profile review in this edition. [Source](https://gitlab.com/ayrsd/audiostellar/-/raw/audiostellar2/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/algomus.fr/perso-ai/-/raw/master/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/libreterrain/libreterrain/-/raw/main/README.md) [Source](https://gitlab.com/ar39907/embedded-amp-model/-/raw/main/README.md)
- **codeberg** · gap: The live Codeberg discovery request was blocked by robots restrictions. No route was used to bypass the restriction. This is a dated access gap, not evidence of an empty ecosystem.
- **sourcehut** · gap: The live SourceHut discovery request was blocked by robots restrictions. No usable current primary project page was returned; historical material is not substituted for a successful current search.
- **packages** · searched: Fresh PyPI primary pages for manimvox 0.2.0 and brandly-cli 0.3.11 supplement package discovery. TensorStack.Media’s current NuGet index adds 0.14.6 relative to the retained snapshot, but a version number alone does not establish a meaningful creative upgrade or a completed setup review. The earlier TensorStack guide remains pending. [Source](https://pypi.org/project/manimvox/0.2.0/) [Source](https://pypi.org/project/brandly-cli/0.3.11/) [Source](https://api.nuget.org/v3-flatcontainer/tensorstack.media/index.json)
- **creative-plugins** · searched: Current primary Interstice plugin documentation was fetched beyond GitHub. New source reviews also cover Krita MCP, Blender Safe MCP, ComfyUI shader/noise nodes, agent-controlled DCC bridges and panorama-to-video reference workflows. Host, model, provider and media rights are separated from each plugin’s software grant. [Source](https://docs.interstice.cloud/) [Source](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp) [Source](https://github.com/civitz/blender-safe-mcp) [Source](https://github.com/AEmotionStudio/ComfyUI-ShaderNoiseKSampler)
- **project-sites** · searched: All 88 observed fields received live primary-site/research context attempts through 92 unique URLs: 91 HTTP successes and one OpenSlides SSL failure. Three raw PDF captures were size-bounded. Most compared text was unchanged; the five changed text snapshots were navigation/count updates or the NuGet version index. Additional model cards, project pages and search results are archived. Limited shells and unchanged context are not new-tool or quality evidence. [Source](https://invoke.ai/) [Source](https://developers.meta.com/vr/blog/accelerate-vr-development-with-ai-and-immersive-web-sdk/) [Source](https://luoprojectpage.github.io/callirewrite/) [Source](https://www.tone3000.com/blog/introducing-the-nam-web-player)
- **research-code** · searched: CalliRewrite, NAH-SRCNN, nvTorchCam and Direct3D have concrete released creative/reconstruction code with full software grants archived. Paper/author results remain distinct from independent reproduction; GPU, robot and model dependencies are disclosed. nn_tilde’s root non-commercial software license prevents a new open-source addition despite its useful audio role. [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2405.14832) [Source](https://github.com/polimi-ispl/nah-srcnn) [Source](https://github.com/NVlabs/nvTorchCam) [Source](https://github.com/acids-ircam/nn_tilde)
- **international** · searched: Chinese, Korean, Portuguese, Japanese and English source searches/documentation extend discovery across music, comics, textile planning, avatars and game assets. SoriSoop’s Korean guide, AssetsCraft’s Portuguese technical guide and Chinese Comfy/DCC examples were read; source language, geography, stars and screenshots do not establish quality. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop) [Source](https://github.com/P3droSoares/assetscraft) [Source](https://github.com/lyp-know-how/dsh-plugin-printing-pattern-v1) [Source](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga)

## NVAI-open

Images & design · Video, animation & film · Audio, music & voice · 3D, reconstruction & assets · Photography, restoration & color · AI agents for code-authored media production

The repository was created on 2026-10-19. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Generating local game/media assets and assembling short scene-based drafts, with different documented model choices on Windows and Apple Silicon.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-06. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** Official screenshots and the photo-to-video example show the author’s workflow; model timings are author measurements and do not certify other machines. [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the official Windows setup or signed/notarized Mac DMG. Windows setup supplies its own Python/ComfyUI/PyTorch runtime; on Mac drag the native app into Applications. Download an appropriate separately licensed model in Models before first generation. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Current bounded release, commit and support snapshots were reviewed; this support page returned 2 non-PR issue records, 2 open. This is not a complete blocker audit or proof of sustained support. [Source](https://github.com/mantux775/nvai-open/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** The full project grant is MIT despite GitHub’s NOASSERTION classification. ComfyUI is GPL-3.0 as a separate component. Every model has its own terms; gated FLUX/LTX and restricted MiniMax choices must not be assumed equivalent to the app license. Local hardware, electricity and model storage; initial setup/model downloads need internet. Optional gated models and integrations retain their separate access terms. [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Platform/model capabilities differ. The detailed Mac guide lists 32/64 GB for larger video-with-audio models, beyond the README’s broad 16 GB guidance; follow the model-specific guide. The optional AMD port is experimental, which is not a verdict on the Windows-NVIDIA or native Mac workflow.

### How it uses AI

A local studio connects image, speech, audio, video and image-to-3D models with a scene-based editor and an optional MCP interface. [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)

### Introduction

A local studio connects image, speech, audio, video and image-to-3D models with a scene-based editor and an optional MCP interface. 1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use the official Windows setup or signed/notarized Mac DMG. Windows setup supplies its own Python/ComfyUI/PyTorch runtime; on Mac drag the native app into Applications. Download an appropriate separately licensed model in Models before first generation.
### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source 5](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)

1. Choose the installer and a model supported by the documented platform route.
2. Generate a small image from a prompt before attempting video or 3D.
3. For a Studio project, create scene lanes, generate takes, conform them into the edit and inspect the export.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source 5](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)

- **Hardware:** Windows: 8 GB VRAM workable, 12–16 GB comfortable; approximately 10 GB runtime plus 5–40 GB per model. Mac: 8 GB for small-image/basic short-video routes, 16 GB more comfortable; about 1 GB app plus roughly 10 GB for FLUX.2 Klein. Larger Mac audio/video routes list 32/64 GB. No universal system-RAM minimum is documented for Windows.
- **Software:** Windows 10 2004+/11 64-bit; NVIDIA driver 580.88+ for the documented CUDA route. The Mac app requires Apple Silicon/macOS 15+, while LTX-2.3/2.5 require macOS 26.3. Models are separate downloads.
- **Platforms:** Windows NVIDIA and native Apple Silicon routes are documented. AMD Radeon is explicitly experimental and 3D requires NVIDIA; Linux support is not documented.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/INSTALL.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source 5](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt) [Source 6](https://github.com/Mantux775/NVAI-open/blob/main/docs/STUDIO.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** The full project grant is MIT despite GitHub’s NOASSERTION classification. ComfyUI is GPL-3.0 as a separate component. Every model has its own terms; gated FLUX/LTX and restricted MiniMax choices must not be assumed equivalent to the app license.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. The full project grant is MIT despite GitHub’s NOASSERTION classification. ComfyUI is GPL-3.0 as a separate component. Every model has its own terms; gated FLUX/LTX and restricted MiniMax choices must not be assumed equivalent to the app license.
- **Cost:** Local hardware, electricity and model storage; initial setup/model downloads need internet. Optional gated models and integrations retain their separate access terms.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. ### Limitations

Platform/model capabilities differ. The detailed Mac guide lists 32/64 GB for larger video-with-audio models, beyond the README’s broad 16 GB guidance; follow the model-specific guide. The optional AMD port is experimental, which is not a verdict on the Windows-NVIDIA or native Mac workflow. ## SoriSoop — Local Music Studio

Audio, music & voice · Browser tools & web media

The repository was created on 2026-11-06. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Drafting backing tracks and original vocal sketches with saved lyrics, seeds and settings, then inspecting/exporting WAV files.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-06. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The author reports actual 20/60-second instrumental and 30-second vocal generations on an M4/16 GB Mac. These are owner observations; they do not guarantee musical quality or other-device speed. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the Apple-Silicon DMG or the current Windows x64 setup EXE. Windows 10 version 2004+/11 is documented; the installer supplies WebView2 and Visual C++ prerequisites when needed. First launch prepares isolated engines and model downloads. Source use requires Python 3.11+ and uv. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Current bounded release, commit and support snapshots were reviewed; this support page returned 0 non-PR issue records, 0 open. This is not a complete blocker audit or proof of sustained support. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT covers the app. Stable Audio uses Stability AI Community License conditions; ACE-Step, translation model, fonts and execution components retain separate terms. Check THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt and original model cards. Generation is local after downloads; model-use restrictions remain separate from free application code. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Installers are currently unsigned/not notarized. Song generation can be slow on CPU. The app has no MP3 conversion or automatic lyric writing, and the model’s maximum duration is different from a device recommendation.

### How it uses AI

A Korean-language local music application combines Stable Audio background generation, ACE-Step lyric-based songs and offline Korean-to-English prompt translation. [Source](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Korean-language local music application combines Stable Audio background generation, ACE-Step lyric-based songs and offline Korean-to-English prompt translation. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Drafting backing tracks and original vocal sketches with saved lyrics, seeds and settings, then inspecting/exporting WAV files. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The author reports actual 20/60-second instrumental and 30-second vocal generations on an M4/16 GB Mac. These are owner observations; they do not guarantee musical quality or other-device speed. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Use the Apple-Silicon DMG or the current Windows x64 setup EXE. Windows 10 version 2004+/11 is documented; the installer supplies WebView2 and Visual C++ prerequisites when needed. First launch prepares isolated engines and model downloads. Source use requires Python 3.11+ and uv. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use the Apple-Silicon DMG or the current Windows x64 setup EXE. Windows 10 version 2004+/11 is documented; the installer supplies WebView2 and Visual C++ prerequisites when needed. First launch prepares isolated engines and model downloads. Source use requires Python 3.11+ and uv.

```sh
python3 app.py
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

1. Prepare the chosen instrumental or song model through the first-run dialog.
2. Enter a short original style/lyrics prompt and review the automatically suggested length/settings.
3. Generate one test, listen for pronunciation and transitions and export WAV; saved results and settings remain in the user data directory.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** First preparation requires internet and at least 16 GB free disk. The documented measured Mac had 16 GB unified memory; that measurement does not establish a universal minimum.
- **Software:** Self-contained downloads need no separate Python/Git. Source server: Python 3.11+ and uv for engine setup. The Windows installer bundles the Python runtime plus WebView2/Visual C++ prerequisite installers. Background model about 1.8 GB, song weights about 7.6 GB, optional translated INT8 model about 79 MiB.
- **Platforms:** Apple Silicon/macOS 14+ and Windows 10 version 2004+/11 x64 builds are documented. Windows v1.0.0 generation is CPU-only; Windows song performance and M1/8 GB remain unverified.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT covers the app. Stable Audio uses Stability AI Community License conditions; ACE-Step, translation model, fonts and execution components retain separate terms. Check THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt and original model cards.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT covers the app. Stable Audio uses Stability AI Community License conditions; ACE-Step, translation model, fonts and execution components retain separate terms. Check THIRD_PARTY_NOTICES.txt and original model cards.
- **Cost:** Generation is local after downloads; model-use restrictions remain separate from free application code.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

Installers are currently unsigned/not notarized. Song generation can be slow on CPU. The app has no MP3 conversion or automatic lyric writing, and the model’s maximum duration is different from a device recommendation. [Source 1](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/creatorjosephkr/sori-soop/blob/main/README.md)

This guide does not transfer the author’s production-quality wording into a recommendation. - **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Apache-2.0 covers the Python software. LTX-2.5 uses separate LTX-2.x Community model terms, LTX-2.3 another model license, and the text encoder/additional models have their own terms. Open code does not mean unrestricted model weights. Local compute, substantial model storage and any optional hosted API costs; gated-model access and its terms are separate. [Source](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/packages/ltx-pipelines/docs/installation.md) [Source](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/packages/ltx-pipelines/docs/optimization.md) [Source](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/LICENSE-2_x) [Source](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03233)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The quick-start download is about 66 GiB. DFR needs additional components and more memory; mixing full and distilled models or layouts is invalid. A universal RAM/VRAM minimum was not established from the reviewed instructions. User reports include unwanted text artifacts in LTX-2.5 and motion-related face smearing in a specific LTX-2.3/AMD/ComfyUI run. These reports concern distinct workflows, not a universal hardware or quality verdict.

### How it uses AI

The official Python implementation runs LTX audio/video diffusion pipelines, including text/image conditioning, retakes and specialized LoRA paths.
```sh
git clone https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2.git
```


```sh
cd LTX-2
```


```sh
uv sync --frozen
```


```sh
uv run python -m ltx_pipelines.distilled --help
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/packages/ltx-pipelines/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/packages/ltx-pipelines/docs/optimization.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/LICENSE-2_x) [Source 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03233)

1. Start with the README’s DistilledPipeline recipe and its exact component paths.
2. Use a short prompt, fixed seed and a small documented frame count; do not mix legacy monolithic paths with split-component flags.
3. Inspect the encoded audio/video before exploring DFR, retakes, dubbing or larger outputs.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/packages/ltx-pipelines/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/packages/ltx-pipelines/docs/optimization.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/Lightricks/LTX-2/blob/main/LICENSE-2_x) [Source 5](https://arxiv.org/abs/2601.03233)

- **Software:** uv/Python and the checked package environment; current workspace metadata declares torch>=2.3.1, with additional platform-specific pins. The model component set and optional compiled kernels/attention backend must match the chosen pipeline.
- **Platforms:** Linux/CUDA is the documented natten/compiled-kernel route. The README describes fallback neighborhood attention on Windows/macOS; that installation path does not establish every model’s device support or memory fit.

## Swarm

Computational art & creative coding · Interactive, immersive & live media · Browser tools & web media · Images & design · Performance, projection & stage media

The repository was created on 2026-11-06. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Making interactive particle interpretations of artwork, sound-reactive sketches and short PNG/WebM studies in a browser.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-06. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/bernhardbrugger/swarm/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** A live project site and screenshots show particle drawings and a rotated relief. The default brightness-based depth is deterministic; select AI depth to use the learned model. [Source](https://github.com/bernhardbrugger/swarm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://bernhardbrugger.github.io/swarm/) [Source](https://huggingface.co/onnx-community/depth-anything-v2-small)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Use the static project page or obtain the single-file source. For local camera/microphone access, serve it on localhost rather than relying on a file URL. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/bernhardbrugger/swarm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://bernhardbrugger.github.io/swarm/) [Source](https://huggingface.co/onnx-community/depth-anything-v2-small)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Current bounded release, commit and support snapshots were reviewed; this support page returned 0 non-PR issue records, 0 open. This is not a complete blocker audit or proof of sustained support. [Source](https://github.com/bernhardbrugger/swarm/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT covers Swarm. The selected Depth Anything V2 Small conversion and Transformers.js are described as Apache-2.0; web fonts carry separate SIL OFL notices. This does not grant the same terms to other Depth Anything variants. No account or paid service is documented. Local rendering uses device resources; optional AI/runtime/font downloads contact their CDNs/hosts. [Source](https://github.com/bernhardbrugger/swarm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://bernhardbrugger.github.io/swarm/) [Source](https://huggingface.co/onnx-community/depth-anything-v2-small)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Particle depth is a relative image interpretation, not a metrically accurate scan. Performance adapts by reducing particle count/resolution; no minimum RAM/VRAM or target-device frame-rate guarantee is documented.

### How it uses AI

Combines a WebGL particle instrument with optional on-device Depth Anything V2 Small inference to turn images/text/webcam input into animated relief. [Source](https://github.com/bernhardbrugger/swarm/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://bernhardbrugger.github.io/swarm/) [Source](https://huggingface.co/onnx-community/depth-anything-v2-small)

### Introduction

Combines a WebGL particle instrument with optional on-device Depth Anything V2 Small inference to turn images/text/webcam input into animated relief. ## P-JiT MLX

Images & design · Mobile, edge & on-device creation · Computational art & creative coding

The repository was created on 2026-11-05. This is its first full guide in this library, not independent validation of a new release.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Generating dense-caption image studies locally while comparing precision, seeded composition and memory tradeoffs.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-06. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The author reports fifteen seeded-gallery comparisons and M4 Pro/48 GB measurements. These are port-author checks, not independent validation; 8-bit can change composition on some seeds. [Source](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Install the project Python package on an Apple-Silicon MLX-capable system. Choose bf16 or 8bit and let the matching published export download on first use; use a dense original caption and start with one image. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Current bounded release, commit and support snapshots were reviewed; this page returned 0 non-PR issue records. This is not a complete blocker audit or proof of sustained support. [Source](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** Apache-2.0 covers the code. The README identifies P-JiT and Qwen weights as Apache-2.0, with separate NOTICE/model-card attribution. Quantized conversions and input images still need their own provenance. Local generation after model downloads; optional OpenRouter prompt upsampling incurs separate service access/usage. [Source](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. The model cannot render text reliably and struggles with full-body figures/crowds. Quantization does not guarantee seed parity; process peak memory is not a universal system-RAM minimum. Optional --upsample sends the caption to Claude through OpenRouter.

### How it uses AI

An Apple-Silicon MLX implementation runs Linum P-JiT 21K text-to-image generation with a Qwen caption encoder and optional quantized exports. [Source](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

An Apple-Silicon MLX implementation runs Linum P-JiT 21K text-to-image generation with a Qwen caption encoder and optional quantized exports. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

Generating dense-caption image studies locally while comparing precision, seeded composition and memory tradeoffs. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

### Demo & examples

The author reports fifteen seeded-gallery comparisons and M4 Pro/48 GB measurements. These are port-author checks, not independent validation; 8-bit can change composition on some seeds. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install the project Python package on an Apple-Silicon MLX-capable system. Choose bf16 or 8bit and let the matching published export download on first use; use a dense original caption and start with one image. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install the project Python package on an Apple-Silicon MLX-capable system. Choose bf16 or 8bit and let the matching published export download on first use; use a dense original caption and start with one image.

```sh
pip install git+https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx
```


```sh
pjit-mlx --tier 8bit --low-memory --seeds 4 --aspect 4:3 --out study.png --prompt "A quiet still life of blue ceramic vessels beside a sunlit window"
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

1. Choose reference bf16 when memory permits, or 8-bit/low-memory for a smaller experiment.
2. Generate one image and compare composition/detail before increasing the batch.
3. Save prompt, seed, dimensions and precision tier; optional prompt upsampling is a separate hosted call.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Author measurements on M4 Pro/48 GB: about 6.7 GB whole-process peak for bf16; about 5.0 GB for 8-bit and 3.6 GB with low-memory in the stated example. Downloads about 11.9 GB/bf16, 6.9 GB/8-bit, or 18 GB for conversion from released checkpoints. No universal minimum is inferred.
- **Software:** Python package environment with MLX on Apple Silicon; exact minimum Python/macOS versions are not stated in the reviewed README. FFmpeg and a separate video toolchain are not described as requirements for this image route.
- **Platforms:** Apple Silicon/macOS is the documented platform. Intel Macs, Windows and Linux are not established by this MLX port.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

- **Code:** Apache-2.0
- **Weights:** Apache-2.0 covers the code. The README identifies P-JiT and Qwen weights as Apache-2.0, with separate NOTICE/model-card attribution. Quantized conversions and input images still need their own provenance.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows Apache-2.0. Apache-2.0 covers the code. The README identifies P-JiT and Qwen weights as Apache-2.0, with separate NOTICE/model-card attribution. Quantized conversions and input images still need their own provenance.
- **Cost:** Local generation after model downloads; optional OpenRouter prompt upsampling incurs separate service access/usage.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

### Limitations

The model cannot render text reliably and struggles with full-body figures/crowds. Quantization does not guarantee seed parity; process peak memory is not a universal system-RAM minimum. Optional --upsample sends the caption to Claude through OpenRouter. [Source 1](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/felix-ab/pjit-mlx/blob/main/README.md)

## Poseforge

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Developing a reference character, manipulating its rig, generating pose-conditioned stills and stitching short video segments.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-06. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The screenshots use an off-the-shelf rigged GLB to demonstrate editing. They do not prove that the shown character was generated through Meshy. [Source](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/blob/main/README.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** Clone the source, install npm packages and run the development server at port 3000. Configure your own billing-enabled Gemini project and Meshy API access for the generation stages. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/blob/main/README.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Current bounded release, commit and support snapshots were reviewed; this support page returned 0 non-PR issue records, 0 open. This is not a complete blocker audit or proof of sustained support. [Source](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT covers the code. Bundled motion content has its own CMU database notice. Gemini, Meshy and any imported World Labs assets retain separate service/content terms. Every requested mesh, rig, generated image and video uses your own paid provider access. Published cost hints are estimates rather than a budget guarantee. [Source](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/blob/main/README.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. Local-first storage does not mean local model inference: requested media is sent to providers. Hosting is deferred, and the illustrated GLB is not a validated result of the paid generation path.

### How it uses AI

Uses Gemini image/video generation and Meshy mesh/rig services, with a local browser pose editor and keyframe timeline. [Source](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

Uses Gemini image/video generation and Meshy mesh/rig services, with a local browser pose editor and keyframe timeline. [Source 1](https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge/blob/main/README.md)

```sh
git clone https://github.com/wolfgangjblack/poseforge
```


```sh
cd poseforge
```


```sh
npm install
```


```sh
npm run dev
```

### What it is good for

Making a short manga or webtoon with a shared character/style bible, then exporting an editable production project and PDF or image ZIP. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

### Demo & examples

The README includes pages from the author-produced Wei Zheng Slays the Dragon King comic and screenshots of the storyboard and visual composer. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

### Install

Use Python 3.10+ and Node.js 20+. Install backend requirements and frontend packages, build the frontend, then start the server on port 5180. Windows also has a run.bat launcher. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Use Python 3.10+ and Node.js 20+. Install backend requirements and frontend packages, build the frontend, then start the server on port 5180. Windows also has a run.bat launcher.

```sh
pip install -r backend/requirements.txt
```


```sh
npm --prefix frontend install
```


```sh
npm --prefix frontend run build
```


```sh
python -m backend.main 5180
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

1. Create a project and a short storyboard; set character and location prompt blocks.
2. Configure an OpenAI-compatible language endpoint and optional SwarmUI at port 7801; generate or upload each panel.
3. Adjust frames and dialogue, render page previews, correct the results and export PDF/ZIP.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Python 3.10+, Node.js 20+, FastAPI and the published backend requirements. SwarmUI and an LLM endpoint are optional separate services for the AI stages; ONNX Runtime supports the optional Real-ESRGAN upscale route.
- **Platforms:** Windows launcher and Python/npm setup are documented. A verified macOS/Linux compatibility matrix is not published.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

- **Code:** MIT
- **Weights:** MIT covers this pipeline. SwarmUI checkpoints, LoRAs, the chosen LLM and optional upscaling weights have separate terms; paid API use is optional and separately billed.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows MIT. MIT covers this pipeline. SwarmUI checkpoints, LoRAs, the chosen LLM and optional upscaling weights have separate terms; paid API use is optional and separately billed.
- **Cost:** Local compute plus any chosen external model or service; provider access and charges are separate.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

### Limitations

Character consistency and print quality still need human inspection. Generated page dimensions alone do not establish a print-production proof or a quality recommendation. [Source 1](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/main/backend/requirements.txt)

### Get the tool

- [Repository](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga)
- [Documentation](https://github.com/TTDiang2/TT_AutoManga/blob/master/README.md)

## OpenSuno YuE2 Studio

Audio, music & voice · Browser tools & web media

First complete guide in this library, including completion of any earlier screened entry; not a claim of a launch today.

### Quality assessment: Quality unverified

Software licensing and a concrete creative AI use are supported. The remaining checks do not establish a recommendation.

Scope: Prototyping songs or instrumentals from lyrics and a style, comparing saved seeds and re-rendering a chosen arrangement.

Evidence checked: 2026-11-06. No local execution implied.

- **Open-source software license · verified:** The complete root software license was read and archived. The grant covers this project code; unresolved model, service and asset terms are disclosed separately. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/LICENSE)
- **Creative results · documented:** The README documents the song library and playback/edit workflow. A cover is a new performance of a transcription, not preservation of the original singer; a duration cap may end abruptly. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/START-HERE.md)
- **Reproducible setup · documented:** On Apple Silicon use the project DMG installer or the documented Install OpenSuno.command source route, then Launch Studio.command. Download the selected generator from Models. The source route isolates MLX generation from PyTorch cover analysis. Independent reproduction has not been established in this review. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/START-HERE.md)
- **Maintenance and support · unknown:** Current bounded release, commit and support snapshots were reviewed; this support page returned 0 non-PR issue records, 0 open. This is not a complete blocker audit or proof of sustained support. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/opensuno-yue2/issues)
- **Independent use · unknown:** Targeted live web searches and current repository support evidence were reviewed. No independent successful use was established for this selected workflow; directory listings, copied demos and author claims are not sufficient.
- **Models, services and costs · documented:** MIT covers OpenSuno code only. The model guide labels listed weights CC BY-NC 4.0 unless a model card says otherwise; LoRAs have individual terms. The source license does not grant commercial model or generated-music rights. Local generation uses your hardware after downloads. Optional OpenAI requests require separate paid access. Non-commercial weight conditions remain separate from software cost. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/START-HERE.md)

Documentation review only; AI Media Scout did not install or run this software. No universal minimum RAM/VRAM is published. Tested M4 Pro/64 GB and RTX 4070 Ti SUPER/16 GB configurations are examples. Windows experimental support does not establish a verdict for the scoped Mac workflow. Optional OpenAI art/style requests and LRCLIB search contact external services.

### How it uses AI

A local browser studio for YuE2 song generation, score-guided covers, continuation and arrangement edits, with separate Apple-Silicon and NVIDIA render paths. [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/START-HERE.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. On Apple Silicon use the project DMG installer or the documented Install OpenSuno.command source route, then Launch Studio.command. Download the selected generator from Models. The source route isolates MLX generation from PyTorch cover analysis.

```sh
bash "scripts/Install OpenSuno.command"
```


```sh
bash "scripts/Launch Studio.command"
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/START-HERE.md)

1. Open localhost:7862 and download YuE2 BF16 on a Mac, or the matching CUDA package on a supported render node.
2. Enter original lyrics/section tags and a style, choose a short duration and save a seed.
3. Compare versions, inspect transitions and export WAV/MP3; use only recordings and model adapters whose terms permit the intended use.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/MODELS.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/victorcampeanu/OpenSuno-YuE2/blob/main/docs/START-HERE.md)

- **Hardware:** Leave about 20 GB free for weights/environments. Mac generator is about 7.5 GB; CUDA generator about 7.8 GB; optional cover analysis about 2.8 GB and continuation tokenizer about 0.3 GB. Minimum RAM/VRAM is not documented.
- **Software:** Apple-Silicon MLX route uses separate Python 3.12/3.11 environments and FFmpeg supplied by the installer. Windows uses 64-bit Python 3.11 with the py launcher, FFmpeg on PATH and a compatible NVIDIA driver. Studio runs at port 7862; render node at 7863.
- **Platforms:** Apple Silicon/macOS is the main local route. Windows/NVIDIA is explicitly experimental pending longer-generation and cover validation; other devices may use an authenticated remote node, which is different from local generation support.

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/alt-key-project/comfyui-dream-project/blob/master/readme.md)

### Limitations

The author documents FFmpeg argument incompatibilities and variable-frame-rate surprises in mpegCoder. Sequence nodes must not be placed in parallel. These specific caveats require testing rather than implying all workflows are Experimental. [Source 1](https://github.com/alt-key-project/comfyui-dream-project/blob/master/readme.md)

### How it uses AI

Connects an MCP-capable model/agent to a Krita plugin for canvas operations, image inspection and optional configured image/video generation backends. [Source](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

### Introduction

Connects an MCP-capable model/agent to a Krita plugin for canvas operations, image inspection and optional configured image/video generation backends. [Source 1](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

Iterating on layered illustrations, retouching, inpainting and animation while preserving a reviewable Krita document. [Source 1](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

[Source 1](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

### Install

Import the release bridge ZIP through Krita’s Python-plugin importer, enable it and restart. Install the Python MCP server, open the AI Bridge docker and copy its client configuration. Configure model backends separately when needed. [Source 1](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Import the release bridge ZIP through Krita’s Python-plugin importer, enable it and restart. Install the Python MCP server, open the AI Bridge docker and copy its client configuration. Configure model backends separately when needed.

```sh
pip install .
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

1. Create a small canvas and connect an MCP-capable client through the panel’s configuration.
2. Ask for a bounded layered painting/edit, inspect the result and use the documented undo/pause controls.
3. Enable a chosen generation backend only when its service/model access and costs have been reviewed.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/installation.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/jowinjohnchemban/krita-mcp/blob/main/docs/ai-backends.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Krita 5.2+ with Python-plugin support and Python 3.10+ for the MCP server. The reviewed README tests Krita 5.3.4. ### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/setup.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/models.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/example/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Documented target: NVIDIA GPU with 24 GB VRAM and at least 32 GB system/WSL RAM. The tested host had 94 GB RAM. About 75 GB disk is described for the base setup (54 GB model files); optional edit weights add about 30 GB. Keep hot weights on Linux/ext4 under WSL.
- **Software:** GPU side: Linux or WSL2 Ubuntu 22.04/24.04, Python 3.10+ with venv and a driver supporting CUDA 12.8+ wheels. Tools: Bun 1.1+, FFmpeg with libass/libx264/librubberband and Claude Code login.
- **Platforms:** Windows 11 + WSL2 Ubuntu 24.04 is end-to-end tested by the author. Plain Linux uses the same commands but is explicitly not yet fully run. macOS local GPU generation is not documented.

### License, model weights & costs

The full software license was reviewed. Model weights, assets and services remain separate. [Source 1](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/LICENSE) [Source 2](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/README.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/setup.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/models.md) [Source 5](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/example/README.md)

- **Code:** Apache-2.0
- **Weights:** Apache-2.0 covers this project code. Local model/node-pack licenses differ; the guide reports an additional research-purpose statement for VibeVoice. Review original model cards rather than treating the project’s interpretation as a commercial clearance. Claude is a separate hosted dependency.
- **Commercial:** Software use follows Apache-2.0. Apache-2.0 covers this project code. Local model/node-pack licenses differ; the guide reports an additional research-purpose statement for VibeVoice. Review original model cards rather than treating the project’s interpretation as a commercial clearance. Claude is a separate hosted dependency.
- **Cost:** Local rendering consumes GPU time/storage; the layout-planning step uses your Claude plan/API access. No bundled paid-model entitlement is provided.

### Why it merits attention

Included for the documented creative workflow. A complete guide and popularity do not establish output reliability. [Source 1](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/setup.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/models.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/example/README.md)

### Limitations

Voices can need retries and GPU rendering is slow. The supplied recording is a test pattern, not evidence of a successful real product presentation. Synthetic presenters and voice samples require appropriate consent and disclosure; our review did not run the pipeline. [Source 1](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/README.md) [Source 2](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/setup.md) [Source 3](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/docs/models.md) [Source 4](https://github.com/gjovanov/roomler-ai-avatar/blob/main/example/README.md)

### How it uses AI

A Windows MCP server lets an AI agent build animation/edit timelines, inspect rendered frames and use optional local recognition models for transcripts, blur and cutouts. [Source](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

### Introduction

A Windows MCP server lets an AI agent build animation/edit timelines, inspect rendered frames and use optional local recognition models for transcripts, blur and cutouts. [Source 1](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

### What it is good for

### Demo & examples

The built-in Tora cat animation and contact sheet are code-drawn author demonstrations. Privacy-model and timing claims require checking on the intended footage. [Source 1](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

### Install

Install FFmpeg/ffprobe, download and verify the Windows release, then install its MCP bundle or connect the extracted executable as a stdio MCP server. Published builds are self-contained; .NET SDK is for source development. [Source 1](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

1. Obtain the official source or release linked below.
2. Install FFmpeg/ffprobe, download and verify the Windows release, then install its MCP bundle or connect the extracted executable as a stdio MCP server. Published builds are self-contained; .NET SDK is for source development.

```sh
winget install Gyan.FFmpeg
```

### First project

Start with a short, representative project and inspect the result before a larger batch. [Source 1](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

2. Create a short project from owned assets, inspect contact sheets and edit timing before a larger render.
3. Regenerate captions after later cuts; inspect the MP4 or export the timeline as OTIO/FCPXML for finishing.
### Hardware & software

Requirements below are documented for this workflow. Unspecified limits are not inferred. [Source 1](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

- **Hardware:** Minimum system RAM, VRAM and total disk space are not documented in the reviewed instructions.
- **Software:** Windows 10/11 x64 and FFmpeg/ffprobe. .NET 10 SDK only for building/testing. Optional whisper.cpp plus a ggml model enables word-timed transcription; local AI weights download on first use.
- **Platforms:** Windows x64 is the documented target. macOS/Linux runtime support is not established by timeline export to editors on those systems.

One project is open at a time; undo is session memory and previews are reduced resolution. Whisper word times remain approximate, and export is H.264/AAC MP4. The exe is currently not code-signed. [Source 1](https://github.com/zeyarwinthu/zeyocut/blob/main/README.md)

### Get the tool

## Excluded and unresolved findings

Research notes only; these do not enter the eligible tool library.

- **JGRFW/comfyui-AICG3D-CSR** · needs-license-review: The root grants MIT, but LEGAL.md also states repository-wide use restrictions. The scope of those additional conditions needs clarification before calling the complete software terms verified open-source. [Source](https://github.com/JGRFW/comfyui-AICG3D-CSR)
- **kaptaindieter/CutCraft** · needs-license-review: The README claims Apache/MIT dual licensing while the current root LICENSE is AGPLv3. The conflicting scope of these statements requires clarification before publishing a verified license label. [Source](https://github.com/kaptaindieter/CutCraft)
- **Fanqz1010/AudioDoctor** · excluded: The current guide describes deterministic/heuristic analysis and DSP repair of AI-generated audio. Processing AI output is not itself an implemented learned-AI contribution. [Source](https://github.com/Fanqz1010/AudioDoctor)
- **kanaworksai-hue/kirapaint** · excluded: The reviewed effect is deterministic Blender material/geometry processing. AI-assisted installation and the ability to accept AI-made meshes do not establish a released AI generation/control component. [Source](https://github.com/kanaworksai-hue/kirapaint)
- **Blizaine/Maestro** · excluded: The root software notice invokes a non-commercial evaluation license and restricts commercial use; it does not qualify as open-source software. [Source](https://github.com/Blizaine/Maestro)
- **HuiYingChung/Openhall** · excluded: Current source/asset notice reserves rights and restricts source/viewer/template reuse, despite the creative exhibition workflow. [Source](https://github.com/HuiYingChung/Openhall)
- **acids-ircam/nn_tilde** · excluded: The complete root software license is Creative Commons Attribution-NonCommercial 4.0. This non-commercial code grant does not meet the project’s open-source-only eligibility rule. [Source](https://github.com/acids-ircam/nn_tilde)
- **lamooon/muse** · needs-license-review: Music transcription is relevant, but no complete software license was returned by the current official license request. Do not infer permission from a public repository. [Source](https://github.com/lamooon/muse)
- **im4vk/HandwritingWithAI** · needs-license-review: The current source review did not return a complete software license. Learned handwriting relevance alone does not establish open-source eligibility. [Source](https://github.com/im4vk/HandwritingWithAI)
- **Manuekle/focus** · needs-license-review: The official license request did not return a software grant; keep the promising editing workflow local pending verification. [Source](https://github.com/Manuekle/focus)
- **aregrid/frame** · excluded: Current recursive tree contains documentation, configuration and screenshots but no implementation files for the advertised AI video editor. Creative functionality is not established; an issue asking whether it is a mockup supplies no maintainer validation. [Source](https://github.com/aregrid/frame)
- **enpts/sho** · excluded: The reviewed brush engine provides deterministic browser painting. Agent-friendly integration alone did not establish a released concrete AI creative contribution in this review. [Source](https://github.com/enpts/sho)
